Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the bayesian structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 574. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 934 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 574 + O(1/n^934). Summing over all modes and applying the bayesian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 219.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)
